```json
    {
      "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-inc-densities",
        "exact_quote": "Define the following marginal probability measures of $\\mathsf H$: $$\\begin{split}\n \\mathsf H_A(d\\mathbf s,dA)\n   &=\\mu^{\\otimes k}(d\\mathbf s)\\,\\gamma_U(dA),\\\\\n \\mathsf H_C(d\\mathbf s,dB,dC)\n   &=\\mu^{\\otimes k}(d\\mathbf s)\\,\\gamma_{p,d}(dB)\\,\\upsilon_{BU}(dC).\n \\end{split}$$ Using the unnormalized measures of <a href=\"#inc:fiber-measure\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"inc:fiber-measure\">[inc:fiber-measure]</a>, define nonnegative measures on the corresponding exact incidence sets by $$\\begin{aligned}\n \\mathsf M_A(d\\mathbf s,dA)\n  &=\\mu(ds_1)\\gamma_{n,d}(dA)\n       \\prod_{i=2}^k\\lambda_{A,As_1}(ds_i),\\label{inc:MA}\\\\\n \\mathsf M_C(d\\mathbf s,dB,dC)\n  &=\\mu(ds_1)\\gamma_{p,d}(dB)\\upsilon_{n,p}(dC)\n       \\prod_{i=2}^k\\lambda_{D,Ds_1}(ds_i),\\qquad D=CB.\\label{inc:MC}\n\\end{aligned}$$ Values at row-rank failures may be set to zero. The weights on the right below are assigned arbitrary finite values on their Gram-rank null sets. These measures satisfy the exact identities $$\\begin{aligned}\n \\mathsf M_A\n  &=(2\\pi)^{-n\\ell/2}\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}U)^{-n/2}\\,\\mathsf H_A,\n       \\label{inc:undivided-A}\\\\\n \\mathsf M_C\n  &=\\kappa\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}B^{\\mathsf T}BU)^{-n/2}\\,\\mathsf H_C,\n \\qquad\n \\kappa=\\frac{|\\mathcal V_{n,p-\\ell}|}{|\\mathcal V_{n,p}|}.\n       \\label{inc:undivided-C}\n\\end{aligned}$$ These are equalities of nonnegative measures.*\n\n\n\n*For $L=A,D$, the values $p_L(Ls_1)$ are positive and finite $\\mathsf H$-almost surely and equal the shrinking-ball limits $$p_L(Ls_1)=\\lim_{j\\to\\infty}\n \\frac{\\mu\\{z:\\|Lz-Ls_1\\|<2^{-j}\\}}{v_n2^{-jn}}.$$ On this full-measure set, the densities of $Q$ and $R$ with respect to $\\mathsf H$ are $$\\begin{aligned}\n q_*&=w\\phi_J(A,As_1)(2\\pi)^{-n\\ell/2}\n       \\det(U^{\\mathsf T}U)^{-n/2}p_A(As_1)^{-\\ell},\n       \\label{inc:q-density}\\\\\n r_*&=\\kappa\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}B^{\\mathsf T}BU)^{-n/2}\n       p_D(Ds_1)^{-\\ell}.\\label{inc:r-density}\n\\end{aligned}$$ In particular $Q\\ll R$.",
        "source_id": "PM-140.2"
      },
      "evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "stmt-inc-densities",
          "exact_quote": "Define the following marginal probability measures of $\\mathsf H$: $$\\begin{split}\n \\mathsf H_A(d\\mathbf s,dA)\n   &=\\mu^{\\otimes k}(d\\mathbf s)\\,\\gamma_U(dA),\\\\\n \\mathsf H_C(d\\mathbf s,dB,dC)\n   &=\\mu^{\\otimes k}(d\\mathbf s)\\,\\gamma_{p,d}(dB)\\,\\upsilon_{BU}(dC).\n \\end{split}$$ Using the unnormalized measures of <a href=\"#inc:fiber-measure\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"inc:fiber-measure\">[inc:fiber-measure]</a>, define nonnegative measures on the corresponding exact incidence sets by $$\\begin{aligned}\n \\mathsf M_A(d\\mathbf s,dA)\n  &=\\mu(ds_1)\\gamma_{n,d}(dA)\n       \\prod_{i=2}^k\\lambda_{A,As_1}(ds_i),\\label{inc:MA}\\\\\n \\mathsf M_C(d\\mathbf s,dB,dC)\n  &=\\mu(ds_1)\\gamma_{p,d}(dB)\\upsilon_{n,p}(dC)\n       \\prod_{i=2}^k\\lambda_{D,Ds_1}(ds_i),\\qquad D=CB.\\label{inc:MC}\n\\end{aligned}$$ Values at row-rank failures may be set to zero. The weights on the right below are assigned arbitrary finite values on their Gram-rank null sets. These measures satisfy the exact identities $$\\begin{aligned}\n \\mathsf M_A\n  &=(2\\pi)^{-n\\ell/2}\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}U)^{-n/2}\\,\\mathsf H_A,\n       \\label{inc:undivided-A}\\\\\n \\mathsf M_C\n  &=\\kappa\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}B^{\\mathsf T}BU)^{-n/2}\\,\\mathsf H_C,\n \\qquad\n \\kappa=\\frac{|\\mathcal V_{n,p-\\ell}|}{|\\mathcal V_{n,p}|}.\n       \\label{inc:undivided-C}\n\\end{aligned}$$ These are equalities of nonnegative measures.*\n\n\n\n*For $L=A,D$, the values $p_L(Ls_1)$ are positive and finite $\\mathsf H$-almost surely and equal the shrinking-ball limits $$p_L(Ls_1)=\\lim_{j\\to\\infty}\n \\frac{\\mu\\{z:\\|Lz-Ls_1\\|<2^{-j}\\}}{v_n2^{-jn}}.$$ On this full-measure set, the densities of $Q$ and $R$ with respect to $\\mathsf H$ are $$\\begin{aligned}\n q_*&=w\\phi_J(A,As_1)(2\\pi)^{-n\\ell/2}\n       \\det(U^{\\mathsf T}U)^{-n/2}p_A(As_1)^{-\\ell},\n       \\label{inc:q-density}\\\\\n r_*&=\\kappa\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}B^{\\mathsf T}BU)^{-n/2}\n       p_D(Ds_1)^{-\\ell}.\\label{inc:r-density}\n\\end{aligned}$$ In particular $Q\\ll R$.",
          "source_id": "PM-140.2"
        }
      ],
      "exact_statement": "Define the following marginal probability measures of $\\mathsf H$: $$\\begin{split}\n \\mathsf H_A(d\\mathbf s,dA)\n   &=\\mu^{\\otimes k}(d\\mathbf s)\\,\\gamma_U(dA),\\\\\n \\mathsf H_C(d\\mathbf s,dB,dC)\n   &=\\mu^{\\otimes k}(d\\mathbf s)\\,\\gamma_{p,d}(dB)\\,\\upsilon_{BU}(dC).\n \\end{split}$$ Using the unnormalized measures of <a href=\"#inc:fiber-measure\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"inc:fiber-measure\">[inc:fiber-measure]</a>, define nonnegative measures on the corresponding exact incidence sets by $$\\begin{aligned}\n \\mathsf M_A(d\\mathbf s,dA)\n  &=\\mu(ds_1)\\gamma_{n,d}(dA)\n       \\prod_{i=2}^k\\lambda_{A,As_1}(ds_i),\\label{inc:MA}\\\\\n \\mathsf M_C(d\\mathbf s,dB,dC)\n  &=\\mu(ds_1)\\gamma_{p,d}(dB)\\upsilon_{n,p}(dC)\n       \\prod_{i=2}^k\\lambda_{D,Ds_1}(ds_i),\\qquad D=CB.\\label{inc:MC}\n\\end{aligned}$$ Values at row-rank failures may be set to zero. These measures satisfy the exact identities $$\\begin{aligned}\n \\mathsf M_A\n  &=(2\\pi)^{-n\\ell/2}\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}U)^{-n/2}\\,\\mathsf H_A,\n       \\label{inc:undivided-A}\\\\\n \\mathsf M_C\n  &=\\kappa\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}B^{\\mathsf T}BU)^{-n/2}\\,\\mathsf H_C,\n \\qquad\n \\kappa=\\frac{|\\mathcal V_{n,p-\\ell}|}{|\\mathcal V_{n,p}|}.\n       \\label{inc:undivided-C}\n\\end{aligned}$$ These are equalities of nonnegative measures.*\n\n\n\n*For $L=A,D$, the values $p_L(Ls_1)$ are positive and finite $\\mathsf H$-almost surely and equal the shrinking-ball limits $$p_L(Ls_1)=\\lim_{j\\to\\infty}\n \\frac{\\mu\\{z:\\|Lz-Ls_1\\|<2^{-j}\\}}{v_n2^{-jn}}.$$ On this full-measure set, the densities of $Q$ and $R$ with respect to $\\mathsf H$ are $$\\begin{aligned}\n q_*&=w\\phi_J(A,As_1)(2\\pi)^{-n\\ell/2}\n       \\det(U^{\\mathsf T}U)^{-n/2}p_A(As_1)^{-\\ell},\n       \\label{inc:q-density}\\\\\n r_*&=\\kappa\\det(U^{\\mathsf T}B^{\\mathsf T}BU)^{-n/2}\n       p_D(Ds_1)^{-\\ell}.\\label{inc:r-density}\n\\end{aligned}$$ In particular $Q\\ll R$.",

    {
      "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-inc-stiefel",
        "exact_quote": "For $1\\le n\\le h$, with the induced Euclidean matrix metric, $$\\label{inc:stiefel-volume}\n |\\mathcal V_{n,h}|=\n 2^{n(n-1)/4}\\prod_{j=1}^n|\\mathbb S^{h-j}|.$$ Consequently, for <a href=\"#inc:dimensions\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"inc:dimensions\">[inc:dimensions]</a>, $$\\label{inc:kappa}\n \\kappa=\\pi^{-n\\ell/2}\\prod_{j=1}^n\n \\frac{\\Gamma((p-j+1)/2)}{\\Gamma((p-j+1-\\ell)/2)}\n \\ge\\left(\\frac{p-n+1-\\ell}{2\\pi}\\right)^{n\\ell/2}.$$",
        "source_id": "PM-140.2"
      },
      "evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "stmt-inc-stiefel",
          "exact_quote": "For $1\\le n\\le h$, with the induced Euclidean matrix metric, $$\\label{inc:stiefel-volume}\n |\\mathcal V_{n,h}|=\n 2^{n(n-1)/4}\\prod_{j=1}^n|\\mathbb S^{h-j}|.$$ Consequently, for <a href=\"#inc:dimensions\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"inc:dimensions\">[inc:dimensions]</a>, $$\\label{inc:kappa}\n \\kappa=\\pi^{-n\\ell/2}\\prod_{j=1}^n\n \\frac{\\Gamma((p-j+1)/2)}{\\Gamma((p-j+1-\\ell)/2)}\n \\ge\\left(\\frac{p-n+1-\\ell}{2\\pi}\\right)^{n\\ell/2}.$$",
          "source_id": "PM-140.2"
        }
      ],
      "exact_statement": "For $1\\le n\\le h$, with the induced Euclidean matrix metric, $$\\label{inc:stiefel-volume}\n |\\mathcal V_{n,h}|=\n 2^{n(n-1)/4}\\prod_{j=1}^n|\\mathbb S^{h-j}|.$$ Consequently, for <a href=\"#inc:dimensions\" data-reference-type=\"eqref\" data-reference=\"inc:dimensions\">[inc:dimensions]</a>, $$\\label{inc:kappa}\n \\kappa=\\pi^{-n\\ell/2}\\prod_{j=1}^n\n \\frac{\\Gamma((p-j+1)/2)}{\\Gamma((p-j+1-\\ell)/2)}\n \\ge\\left(\\frac{p-n+1-\\ell}{2\\pi}\\right)^{n\\ell/2}.$$",

    {
      "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-inc-kernel",
        "exact_quote": "Under $Q$, define $$\\begin{split}\n K&=\\int\n   \\operatorname{dist}(z-s_1,\\operatorname{col}U)^{-n}\\,d\\mu(z),\\\\\n p_A^*(t)&=\\sup_{\\rho>0}\n   \\frac{\\mu\\{z:\\|Az-t\\|<\\rho\\}}{v_n\\rho^n},\\qquad\n K_0=v_n\\|A\\|_{\\mathrm{op}}^n p_A^*(As_1).\n \\end{split}$$ Then $K_0\\ge2^{-n}$, and $$\\label{inc:kernel-bound}\n K\\le K_0L_F,\\qquad\n L_F=1+\\frac{n}{d-\\ell-n}+n\\log(2/\\tau),\\qquad\n \\tau=\\left(\\frac{2^{-n}}{Fd^d}\\right)^{1/(d-\\ell-n)}.$$ In particular, $\\log L_F\\le C\\log d+C\\log(1+\\log F)$, and $K<\\infty$. Moreover, $$\\label{inc:conditional-density}\n \\mathbb E_Q[p_D(Ds_1)\\mid\\mathbf s,A,y,J]\n \\le (2\\pi)^{-n/2}K .$$",
        "source_id": "PM-140.2"
      },
      "evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "stmt-inc-kernel",
          "exact_quote": "Under $Q$, define $$\\begin{split}\n K&=\\int\n   \\operatorname{dist}(z-s_1,\\operatorname{col}U)^{-n}\\,d\\mu(z),\\\\\n p_A^*(t)&=\\sup_{\\rho>0}\n   \\frac{\\mu\\{z:\\|Az-t\\|<\\rho\\}}{v_n\\rho^n},\\qquad\n K_0=v_n\\|A\\|_{\\mathrm{op}}^n p_A^*(As_1).\n \\end{split}$$ Then $K_0\\ge2^{-n}$, and $$\\label{inc:kernel-bound}\n K\\le K_0L_F,\\qquad\n L_F=1+\\frac{n}{d-\\ell-n}+n\\log(2/\\tau),\\qquad\n \\tau=\\left(\\frac{2^{-n}}{Fd^d}\\right)^{1/(d-\\ell-n)}.$$ In particular, $\\log L_F\\le C\\log d+C\\log(1+\\log F)$, and $K<\\infty$. Moreover, $$\\label{inc:conditional-density}\n \\mathbb E_Q[p_D(Ds_1)\\mid\\mathbf s,A,y,J]\n \\le (2\\pi)^{-n/2}K .$$",
          "source_id": "PM-140.2"
        }
      ],
      "exact_statement": "Under $Q$, define $$\\begin{split}\n K&=\\int\n   \\operatorname{dist}(z-s_1,\\operatorname{col}U)^{-n}\\,d\\mu(z),\\\\\n p_A^*(t)&=\\sup_{\\rho>0}\n   \\frac{\\mu\\{z:\\|Az-t\\|<\\rho\\}}{v_n\\rho^n},\\qquad\n K_0=v_n\\|A\\|_{\\mathrm{op}}^n p_A^*(As_1).\n \\end{split}$$ Then $K_0\\ge2^{-n}$, and $$\\label{inc:kernel-bound}\n K\\le K_0L_F,\\qquad\n L_F=1+\\frac{n}{d-\\ell-n}+n\\log(2/\\tau),\\qquad\n \\tau=\\left(\\frac{2^{-n}}{Fd^d}\\right)^{1/(d-\\ell-n)}.$$ In particular, $\\log L_F\\le C\\log d+C\\log(1+\\log F)$, and $K<\\infty$. Moreover, $$\\label{inc:conditional-density}\n \\mathbb E_Q[p_D(Ds_1)\\mid\\mathbf s,A,y,J]\n \\le (2\\pi)^{-n/2}K .$$",
```

<a id="pm-s2"></a>
## 2. Definitions, validity and bounds

- **Finite-state noiseless regression learner** ([source](#stmt-def-streaming-model)): An unknown unit vector $s\in S^{d-1}$ is fixed before sampling.
- **Total correlation** ([source](#pm-h-12)): For a joint law $Q$ of $(S_1,\ldots,S_t)$ with common marginal $p$, its *total correlation* is $$K=D_{\mathrm{KL}}(Q\Vert p^{\otimes t}).$$

<a id="pm-s3"></a>
## 3. Proof roadmap

- **step-spherical-cap** [claim](#stmt-lem-spherical-ball) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-spherical-ball): Graph over a coordinate patch and compare surface and volume. -> cap mass bound
- **step-residual-information** [claim](#stmt-lem-residual-information) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-residual-information): Binary relative entropy of a success event against a conditionally independent comparison law. -> residual-sphere information lower bound
- **step-replica-inequality** [claim](#stmt-lem-replica-information) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-replica-information): Chain rules before and after projection, subtracted. -> information bounded by entropy plus correlation loss
- **step-equal-label-measure** [claim](#stmt-thm-equal-label-measure) / [proof](#proof-stmt-thm-equal-label-measure): Approximate identities in both integration orders, then bounded reweighting. -> finite equal-label measure identity
- **step-gaussian-test** [claim](#stmt-lem-gaussian-test) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-gaussian-test): Observable likelihood test with unit expectation and entropy variational principle. -> correlation loss bound
- **step-gaussian-density** [claim](#stmt-lem-gaussian-density) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-gaussian-density): Dyadic shells of distances and Jensen averaging in the correct order. -> density comparison bound
- **step-gaussian-block** [claim](#stmt-thm-gaussian-block) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-gaussian-density-2): Combine replica inequality with the test and density comparison. -> block information bound
- **step-gaussian-streaming** [claim](#stmt-prop-gaussian-streaming) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-gaussian-streaming): Iterate the block bound over padded current states and compare with the endpoint bound. -> horizon lower bound
- **step-streaming-theorem** [claim](#stmt-thm-streaming) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-distance-streaming-2): Uniform-prior success implies the weaker success level of the proposition. -> uniform-prior sample lower bound

<a id="pm-s4"></a>
## 4. Engineering Triptych

### Panel 1 - epistemic contract

- **inv-streaming-horizon-lower-bound**: For a finite-state learner with subquadratic memory in bits and a deterministic horizon, constant-probability angular accuracy under the uniform spherical prior forces the number of fresh exact Gaussian measurements to be at least a fixed absolute multiple of the dimension times the logarithm of the inverse accuracy, once the dimension is large enough. The threshold may depend on the memory sequence.
- **inv-block-information-bound**: The conditional information between the signal and a finite message formed from one block of exact Gaussian measurements, given an independent projection revealed only to the analyst, is bounded by the message entropy divided by a quantity proportional to the dimension, plus a term linear in the dimension, plus a doubly logarithmic term in the density bound. The bound is uniform over the prior, the density bound and the message kernel.
- **inv-residual-sphere-information**: Conditional on an independent projection and its exact values, any estimate that is accurate to angular precision epsilon with sufficient probability must carry information of order the residual-sphere dimension times the logarithm of the inverse precision in the state it is generated from.

### Panel 2 - negative knowledge

- **anti-real-register-memory**: Apply the lower bound to a machine whose persistent state is a bank of real-valued registers of unrestricted precision. -> The model counts persistent state in bits through a finite number of state values, so unbounded-precision registers fall outside it and the bound says nothing about them.
- **anti-noisy-labels**: Read the exact-label bound as a consequence or a corollary for streaming regression with noisy labels. -> A bound for noisy labels does not transfer to exact labels and the converse is not asserted either, so the two settings must be treated separately.
- **anti-random-horizon**: Apply the bound to a learner whose stopping time is random with no prescribed deterministic cap. -> The padding and block-counting arguments need a fixed horizon, so an unbounded random stopping time is not covered.

### Panel 3 - reference implementation and oracle

Applicability: `not_applicable`. The paper proves asymptotic information-theoretic lower bounds with unspecified absolute constants and thresholds that hold only for all sufficiently large d, for example the statement that there is an absolute c>0 such that T(d) is at least c d log(1/epsilon(d)). There is no finite procedure computing a bound, and a finite test would not decide any statement quantified over all large dimensions.

<a id="pm-s5"></a>
## 5. Domain engineering contracts

| domain | applies | strength | maturity | basis | why |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| software | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The paper proves lower bounds and gives no procedure or interface that could be coded. It states what no bounded-memory learner can do, not a component to build. |
| games_simulation | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The Gaussian rows and posterior replicas are devices inside the analysis. The paper builds no simulation or game mechanic from them. |
| tools | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | No tool or checker is delivered. The information bounds hold under the stated sphere, Gaussian and finite-state hypotheses, so they cannot be reused as a generic diagnostic. |
| algorithms | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The main results are lower bounds on the sample horizon. Algorithm lower bounds and algorithm designs differ, and the paper gives no matching procedure, so there is no construction to implement. |
| data_structures | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The only stored object is an abstract finite persistent state with a bounded number of values. The paper does not study a layout, query or update cost. |
| hardware_accelerators | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | Memory is counted in bits of abstract state, not in registers, bandwidth or circuits. The paper gives no hardware model. |
| crypto_security | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | No hardness assumption, adversary or security notion appears. The bounds are unconditional information inequalities about a learner, not about a cryptographic scheme. |
| machine_learning | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The result is a theoretical limit for streaming regression with exact labels, a fixed signal and Gaussian rows. It cannot be adapted to noisy or active learning settings without new arguments. |
| optimisation_operations_research | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The paper contains no objective, constraint set or optimisation procedure. Its quantities are mutual informations and success probabilities. |
| distributed_systems | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | Blocks of observations are processed by one learner with one persistent state. The paper does not model nodes, messages between machines or failures. |
| other | False | none | not_applicable | inferred | The value lies in the proof tools: posterior replicas, exact equal-label measures and spherical geometry. These are mathematical devices and give no engineering artefact. |

<a id="pm-s6"></a>
## 6. Applicability limits and search

- **limit-subquadratic-memory** (hypothesis): The sample lower bound is proved only for persistent memory of subquadratic size in the dimension, counted in bits as a finite number of state values. It gives nothing for learners whose memory is quadratic or larger. Blocked use: ['Applying the horizon bound to a learner that stores quadratically many bits or the full raw history', 'Reading the bound as a statement about real-valued registers of unrestricted precision']
- **limit-exact-labels-uniform-prior** (hypothesis): The bound assumes exact noiseless Gaussian labels and success averaged over the uniform spherical prior. Guarantees for noisy labels are a different problem. Blocked use: ['Transferring the exact-label lower bound to noisy-label streaming regression', 'Using it for non-Gaussian designs or learners that choose their own rows']
- **limit-deterministic-horizon** (hypothesis): The learner must stop by a prescribed deterministic horizon. The padding of stopping into absorbing states relies on this cap. Blocked use: ['Applying the bound to a learner with an unbounded random stopping time']
- **limit-large-dimension-threshold** (edge_case): All main conclusions hold only for sufficiently large dimension, with an unspecified absolute constant. The threshold may depend on the memory sequence, so no explicit small-dimension guarantee follows. Blocked use: ['Quoting a concrete numerical sample count for a given moderate dimension', 'Using the constant as an explicit value']
- **limit-bounded-prior-density** (hypothesis): The block bound requires the prior to have a bounded density relative to the uniform sphere. The dependence on the bound is doubly logarithmic, so states of very small probability still carry a controlled but non-zero cost. Blocked use: ['Applying the block bound to priors without a density bound relative to uniform probability on the sphere']
- **limit-nonconstructive-asymptotic** (nonconstructive): The results are asymptotic information-theoretic lower bounds. No algorithm is given, and no matching upper bound is claimed. Blocked use: ['Reading the bound as the cost of a specific estimator', 'Treating the proof as a procedure to implement']

### Formal statement (Lean 4)

Corpus status: formalized in the source corpus (toolchain `leanprover/lean4:v4.34.1`, Mathlib `d13f23b723b8a846827a245b89c10fc7d3f11612`). Not re-checked here; the statements give the exact quantifiers and hypotheses, not a proof.

Scope note of family 140 (`lean/docs/140.md`, verbatim):

> The following describes the scope of the Lean formalization related to the following accompanying paper(s):
>
> - [Memory and precision in noiseless Gaussian regression](../../preprints/Memory-and-precision-in-noiseless-Gaussian-regression-September-27-2026/paper.pdf)
> - [Posterior replicas and conditional information in Gaussian regression](../../preprints/Posterior-replicas-and-conditional-information-in-Gaussian-regression-September-27-2026/paper.pdf)
> - [Localization costs and information growth for exact Gaussian observations](../../preprints/Localization-costs-and-information-growth-for-exact-Gaussian-observations-September-27-2026/paper.pdf)
> - [Projection moments, positive cap domination, and Riesz estimates on the sphere](../../preprints/Projection-moments-positive-cap-domination-and-Riesz-estimates-on-the-sphere-September-27-2026/paper.pdf)
> - [Replacing Gaussian observations in memory-constrained inference](../../preprints/Replacing-Gaussian-observations-in-memory-constrained-inference-September-27-2026/paper.pdf)
> - [Subsphere methods for memory–sample lower bounds in noiseless Gaussian regression](../../preprints/Subsphere-methods-for-memory-sample-lower-bounds-in-noiseless-Gaussian-regression-September-27-2026/paper.pdf)
>
> ## Scope
>
> In noiseless Gaussian regression, a learner observes inner products of an unknown unit vector in $\mathbb R^d$ with independent standard Gaussian vectors. The formalization proves that, for every fixed $A>0$, there is $c_A>0$ such that, in sufficiently large dimension, a learner retaining at most $Ad^2$ bits between observations needs at least $c_A d\log(1/\varepsilon)$ observations to attain angular error at most $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $2/3$ for a uniformly random unit signal, for $0<\varepsilon\le1/10$.
>
> For memory $o(d^2)$, the constant can be universal, and the selected statement also permits success at least $2/3$ separately for every signal. The linked supporting results include inverse Gram-matrix moments, exact-projection densities, regularization bounds, and sphere- and cube-prior block estimates.
>
> The paper studies how posterior replicas—independent signals drawn conditionally on the same observed data—control information in noiseless Gaussian regression. The formalization establishes the equal-label identity for finite measures, its density and measurable forms, and information bounds for Gaussian, distance, Haar, synthetic, and incidence comparisons. These statements retain exact observation labels, independent side randomness, and randomized transition rules.
>
> For a learner using $M(d)=o(d^2)$ bits of memory, the resulting bound says that, for sufficiently large $d$ and $0<\varepsilon\le1/10$, success probability at least $2/3$ under the uniform unit-sphere prior requires $T\ge c d\log(1/\varepsilon)$ observations, where $c>0$ is universal. The formalized streaming result uses the $2/3$ threshold; the stronger $3/5$ variant is outside this scope.
>
> The formalization controls information gained from exact Gaussian observations of a spherical image of the uniform cube. Under the stated entropy and size conditions, repeated localization over $t$ observation blocks produces nested disclosures with mutual information at most $C(n+1)t$ and expected final localization level at most $Ct$. The selected estimates include inverse-volume control, kernel coercivity, and drift for the actual observation rows.
>
> For a law of finite relative entropy with respect to a uniform cell law in sufficiently large dimension, the regularization procedure terminates almost surely in regular cells. Its expected depth and number of attempts are finite, with at most twice one plus the expected depth in expectation; the remaining relative entropy plus $(\log 2)n/4$ times the expected depth is at most the initial relative entropy plus $2/e$.
>
> The paper uses projection moments and domination by positive spherical caps to bound the information carried by exact Gaussian observations of an unknown unit vector. The formalization covers integrated frame and sphere moments, cap domination, Riesz-density estimates with fixed offsets, and bounds valid at every radius. It also covers probability bounds for finite-memory learners at a fixed stopping time and after summing over stopping times.
>
> For $M(d)=o(d^2)$ bits of memory, sufficiently large $d$, and $0<\varepsilon\le1/10$, success probability at least $2/3$ under the uniform sphere prior requires $T\ge c d\log(1/\varepsilon)$ observations for a universal $c>0$. A corresponding bound holds when $M\le A d^2$ for each fixed $A>0$, with the constant allowed to depend on $A$. The randomized learner model allows independent shared seeds; its reduction to a finite-state model is proved for dimensions at least three, with the auxiliary dimension-two case outside this scope.
>
> The paper compares fresh Gaussian observations with observations coupled to information already held in memory. The formalization covers mixed-moment and actual-row estimates, critical-radius, one-level, and two-label comparisons, and conditional-information bounds across a block of observations. For sufficiently large dimension $d$, the relevant entropy bounds of order $d^2$ bound the information in the fresh experiment by that in the coupled experiment plus a term of order $d$; the associated block information increase also has order $d$.
>
> It also covers two-point identities on observation fibers and the fiber-block bound, including the boundary case of $d-2$ observed rows. The uniform unit-sphere prior, Gaussian rows, finite memory states, and randomized transition rules remain part of these statements. The separate streaming consequences and several intermediate comparison constructions are outside this selection.
>
> The paper uses subspheres to bound the number of exact Gaussian observations needed to estimate a unit vector with limited memory. The formalization proves that, for $M(d)=o(d^2)$ bits of memory, sufficiently large $d$, and $0<\varepsilon\le1/10$, success probability at least $2/3$ under the uniform sphere prior, or for every unit signal, requires at least $2^{-16}d\log_2(1/\varepsilon)$ observations. Under the uniform prior, a lower bound of the same order with a universal positive constant holds at success probability $1/2$.
>
> The formalization also covers radius-weighted block and terminal estimates, affine and dimension estimates, and a vector-valued beta-mixture comparison with its boundary cases. These statements retain their dimension, width, radius, and suffix hypotheses, as well as the finite-state learner's independent randomness and restrictions on stopping and output.

```lean
import Mathlib

namespace OAI

open MeasureTheory ProbabilityTheory Set Filter InformationTheory
open scoped ENNReal NNReal RealInnerProductSpace Topology

noncomputable section

namespace CurrentProjection

abbrev Vector (d : ℕ) := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin d)

abbrev Sphere (d : ℕ) := Metric.sphere (0 : Vector d) 1

abbrev Rows (k d : ℕ) := Fin k → Vector d

def uniformSphere (d : ℕ) : Measure (Sphere d) :=
  let σ := (volume : Measure (Vector d)).toSphere
  (σ Set.univ)⁻¹ • σ

def gaussianRows (k d : ℕ) : Measure (Rows k d) :=
  Measure.pi fun _ => stdGaussian (Vector d)

def label {k d : ℕ} (g : Rows k d) (s : Vector d) : Fin k → ℝ :=
  fun i => ⟪s, g i⟫

def LocalGrowth {d : ℕ} (ρ : Measure (Sphere d)) (a : ℕ) (B : ℝ) : Prop :=
  ∀ z : Vector d, ∀ t : ℝ, 0 < t →
    ρ {u | dist (u : Vector d) z ≤ t} ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (B * t ^ a)

def cubeAverage {k d : ℕ} (ρ : Measure (Sphere d)) (g : Rows k d)
    (y : Fin k → ℝ) (δ : ℝ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  ENNReal.ofReal ((2 * δ)⁻¹ ^ k) *
    ρ {u | ∀ i, |label g (u : Vector d) i - y i| ≤ δ}

def exactDensity {k d : ℕ} (ρ : Measure (Sphere d)) (g : Rows k d)
    (y : Fin k → ℝ) : ℝ≥0∞ := by
  classical
  let f := fun j : ℕ => (cubeAverage ρ g y (1 / (j : ℝ))).toReal
  exact if h : ∃ L : ℝ, Tendsto f atTop (𝓝 L) then ENNReal.ofReal h.choose else 0

def AdmissibleDimensions (d m r q : ℕ) : Prop :=
  1 ≤ m ∧ 1 ≤ r ∧ 1 ≤ q ∧ m + r < d - 1 ∧ m + r - m - (q - 1) ≥ 1

def momentConstant (C : ℝ) (d : ℕ) (B D : ℝ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (C * d) * B * (1 + max (Real.log (D / B)) 0))

def MixedMomentMain : Prop :=
  ∃ C : ℝ, 0 < C ∧ ∀ (d m r q : ℕ), AdmissibleDimensions d m r q →
    ∀ (ρ : Measure (Sphere d)), IsFiniteMeasure ρ →
    ∀ (B D : ℝ), 0 < B → 0 < D → LocalGrowth ρ (m+r) B → LocalGrowth ρ (d-1) D →
    (∀ (s : Sphere d) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ →
      (∫⁻ X, (∫⁻ Z, cubeAverage ρ (Fin.append X Z)
        (label (Fin.append X Z) (s : Vector d)) δ ∂gaussianRows r d)^q
          ∂gaussianRows m d) ≤ (momentConstant C d B D)^q) ∧
    (ρ ≪ uniformSphere d → ∃ E : Set (Sphere d), MeasurableSet E ∧ ρ E = 0 ∧
      ∀ s ∉ E,
        (∀ᵐ G ∂gaussianRows (m+r) d,
          Tendsto (fun δ : ℝ => cubeAverage ρ G (label G (s : Vector d)) δ)
            (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 (exactDensity ρ G (label G (s : Vector d))))) ∧
        ((∫⁻ X, (∫⁻ Z, exactDensity ρ (Fin.append X Z)
          (label (Fin.append X Z) (s : Vector d)) ∂gaussianRows r d)^q
            ∂gaussianRows m d) ≤ (momentConstant C d B D)^q) ∧
        (ρ ≠ 0 → ∀ᵐ G ∂gaussianRows (m+r) d,
          0 < exactDensity ρ G (label G (s : Vector d)) ∧
          exactDensity ρ G (label G (s : Vector d)) < ∞))

instance gaussianRows_isProbabilityMeasure (k d : ℕ) : IsProbabilityMeasure (gaussianRows k d) := by
  unfold gaussianRows
  infer_instance

lemma measurable_own_label {k d : ℕ} :
    Measurable (fun z : Rows k d × Sphere d => label z.1 (z.2:Vector d)) := by
  apply Measurable.of_eval
  intro i
  exact (continuous_inner.comp <| (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_snd).prodMk
    ((continuous_apply i).comp continuous_fst)).measurable

def swapLast {A B C : Type*} [MeasurableSpace A] [MeasurableSpace B] [MeasurableSpace C] :
    (A × B) × C ≃ᵐ (A × C) × B where
  toFun p := ((p.1.1,p.2),p.1.2)
  invFun p := ((p.1.1,p.2),p.1.2)
  left_inv _ := rfl
  right_inv _ := rfl
  measurable_toFun := (measurable_fst.fst.prodMk measurable_snd).prodMk measurable_fst.snd
  measurable_invFun := (measurable_fst.fst.prodMk measurable_snd).prodMk measurable_fst.snd

def rowInformation {S W X : Type*} [MeasurableSpace S] [MeasurableSpace W]
    [MeasurableSpace X] (P : Measure ((S×W)×X)) (γ : Measure X) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  klDiv P (P.fst.prod γ)

noncomputable def shannonEntropy {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W] (π : Measure W) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  ∫⁻ w, ENNReal.ofReal (-Real.log (π {w}).toReal) ∂π

def conditionalMutualInformation {Z A B : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Z]
    [MeasurableSpace A] [MeasurableSpace B] [StandardBorelSpace A] [StandardBorelSpace B]
    (P : Measure (Z×(A×B))) [IsFiniteMeasure P] : ℝ≥0∞ := by
  classical
  exact if h : Nonempty (A×B) then
    letI := h
    InformationTheory.klDiv P (P.fst ⊗ₘ (P.condKernel.fst ×ₖ P.condKernel.snd))
  else 0

def actualExperiment {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d × W) × Rows m d)) :
    Measure (((Sphere d × W) × Rows m d) × Rows r d) := P.prod (gaussianRows r d)

instance actualExperiment_isProbabilityMeasure {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] :
    IsProbabilityMeasure (actualExperiment (r:=r) P) :=
  inferInstanceAs (IsProbabilityMeasure (P.prod (gaussianRows r d)))

def actualObservation {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*}
    (p : ((Sphere d × W) × Rows m d) × Rows r d) :
    (Rows (m+r) d × W) × (Fin (m+r) → ℝ) :=
  ((Fin.append p.1.2 p.2,p.1.1.2), label (Fin.append p.1.2 p.2) (p.1.1.1 : Vector d))

instance actualObservation_isProbabilityMeasure {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] :
    IsProbabilityMeasure ((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation) :=
  inferInstance

def selectedCube {k d : ℕ} {W : Type*} (ρ : W → Measure (Sphere d)) (δ : ℝ)
    (z : (Rows k d×W)×(Fin k → ℝ)) : ℝ≥0∞ := cubeAverage (ρ z.1.2) z.1.1 z.2 δ

def selectedExact {k d : ℕ} {W : Type*} (ρ : W → Measure (Sphere d))
    (z : (Rows k d×W)×(Fin k → ℝ)) : ℝ≥0∞ := exactDensity (ρ z.1.2) z.1.1 z.2

def momentBound (C : ℝ) (d : ℕ) (B D : ℝ) : ℝ :=
  Real.exp (C*d)*B*(1+max (Real.log (D/B)) 0)

def logPositive (x : ℝ≥0∞) : ℝ := max (Real.log x.toReal) 0

def logNegative (x : ℝ≥0∞) : ℝ≥0∞ := if x = 0 then ∞ else ENNReal.ofReal (-Real.log x.toReal)

def extendedLogExpectation {A : Type*} [MeasurableSpace A] (μ : Measure A)
    (f : A → ℝ≥0∞) : EReal :=
  ((∫⁻ a, ENNReal.ofReal (logPositive (f a)) ∂μ) : EReal) -
    ((∫⁻ a, logNegative (f a) ∂μ) : EReal)

def PositiveMessage {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W] (π : Measure W) := {w : W // 0 < π {w}}

def completeFamily {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W] {T : Type*} (π : Measure W)
    (f : PositiveMessage π → T) (t₀ : T) (w : W) : T :=
  if h : 0 < π {w} then f ⟨w,h⟩ else t₀

structure ActualRowConclusion {d m r q : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (ρ : W → Measure (Sphere d)) (B D : W → ℝ) : Prop where
  row_finite : rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d) ≠ ∞
  row_le_entropy : (rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal ≤
    (shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd).toReal
  conditional_density : ∀ᵐ gw ∂((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation).fst,
    ((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation).condKernel gw ≪ volume
  actual_limit : ∀ᵐ p ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P,
    Tendsto (fun j : ℕ => selectedCube ρ (1/(j:ℝ)) (actualObservation p)) atTop
      (𝓝 (selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)))
  smoothed_bound : ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ →
    Integrable (fun p => Real.log (1+(selectedCube ρ δ (actualObservation p)).toReal))
      (actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ∧
    (∫ p, Real.log (1+(selectedCube ρ δ (actualObservation p)).toReal) ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ≤
      (rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal/q +
        ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst
  exact_integrable : Integrable (fun p => Real.log (1+(selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)).toReal))
    (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)
  exact_bound : (∫ p, Real.log (1+(selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)).toReal) ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ≤
    (rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal/q +
      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst
  entropy_bound : (rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal/q +
      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst ≤
    (shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd).toReal/q +
      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst
  positive_log_integrable : Integrable (fun p => logPositive (selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)))
    (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)
  extended_log_bound : extendedLogExpectation (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)
    (fun p => selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)) ≤
      (((rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal/q +
        ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst) : EReal)

def actualConditionalRowInformation {d m : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    [StandardBorelSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsFiniteMeasure P] : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  conditionalMutualInformation (P.map (fun p => (p.1.1,(p.2,p.1.2))))

structure ActualRowSourceConclusion {d m r q : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    [StandardBorelSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (ρ : W → Measure (Sphere d)) (B D : W → ℝ) : Prop where
  row_finite : actualConditionalRowInformation P ≠ ∞
  row_le_entropy : (actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal ≤
    (shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd).toReal
  conditional_density : ∀ᵐ gw ∂((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation).fst,
    ((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation).condKernel gw ≪ volume
  actual_limit : ∀ᵐ p ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P,
    Tendsto (fun j : ℕ => selectedCube ρ (1/(j:ℝ)) (actualObservation p)) atTop
      (𝓝 (selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)))
  smoothed_bound : ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ →
    Integrable (fun p => Real.log (1+(selectedCube ρ δ (actualObservation p)).toReal))
      (actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ∧
    (∫ p, Real.log (1+(selectedCube ρ δ (actualObservation p)).toReal) ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ≤
      (actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal/q +
        ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst
  exact_integrable : Integrable (fun p => Real.log (1+(selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)).toReal))
    (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)
  exact_bound : (∫ p, Real.log (1+(selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)).toReal) ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ≤
    (actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal/q +
      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst
  entropy_bound : (actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal/q +
      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst ≤
    (shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd).toReal/q +
      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst
  positive_log_integrable : Integrable (fun p => logPositive (selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)))
    (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)
  extended_log_bound : extendedLogExpectation (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)
    (fun p => selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)) ≤
      (((actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal/q +
        ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst) : EReal)

def alignedExperiment {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows (m+r) d) :=
  (actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map (fun p => (p.1.1,Fin.append p.1.2 p.2))

instance alignedExperiment_probability {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] :
    IsProbabilityMeasure (alignedExperiment (r:=r) P) := by
  unfold alignedExperiment
  infer_instance

def exposedJointMap {d k : ℕ} {W : Type*} (p : (Sphere d×W)×Rows k d) :
    (Rows k d×(Fin k → ℝ))×(Sphere d×W) := ((p.2,label p.2 (p.1.1:Vector d)),p.1)

def exposedInformation {d k : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W] [StandardBorelSpace W]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows k d)) [IsFiniteMeasure P] : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  conditionalMutualInformation (P.map exposedJointMap)

def CriticalComparisonMain : Prop :=
  ∃ (K : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < K ∧ ∀ (d : ℕ), d₀ ≤ d →
    ∀ (W : Type) [MeasurableSpace W] [Countable W] [MeasurableSingletonClass W],
    ∀ (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows (d/10) d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],
    (P.map swapLast).fst = (uniformSphere d).prod (gaussianRows (d/10) d) →
    shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((d:ℝ)^2) →
    exposedInformation (P.fst.prod (gaussianRows (d/10+(d/2-d/10)) d)) ≤
      exposedInformation (alignedExperiment (r:=d/2-d/10) P) + ENNReal.ofReal (K*d)

def OneLevelComparisonMain : Prop :=
  ∃ (C : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < C ∧ ∀ d, d₀ ≤ d →
    ∀ (U : Type) [MeasurableSpace U] [Finite U] [MeasurableSingletonClass U],
    ∀ (P : Measure ((Sphere d×U)×Rows (d/32) d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],
    (P.map swapLast).fst = (uniformSphere d).prod (gaussianRows (d/32) d) →
    exposedInformation (P.fst.prod (gaussianRows (d/32+(d/4-d/32)) d)) ≤
      exposedInformation (alignedExperiment (r:=d/4-d/32) P) +
      ENNReal.ofReal (C*((d:ℝ)+Real.log (1+(d:ℝ)+(shannonEntropy P.fst.snd).toReal)+
        (shannonEntropy P.fst.snd).toReal/(d:ℝ)))

def TwoLabelComparisonMain : Prop :=
  ∃ (C : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < C ∧ ∀ d, d₀ ≤ d →
    ∀ (V : Type) [MeasurableSpace V] [Finite V] [MeasurableSingletonClass V],
    ∀ (P : Measure ((Sphere d×V)×Rows (d/8) d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],
    (P.map swapLast).fst = (uniformSphere d).prod (gaussianRows (d/8) d) →
    shannonEntropy P.fst.snd ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((d:ℝ)^2) →
    exposedInformation (P.fst.prod (gaussianRows (d/8+(d/2-d/8)) d)) ≤
      exposedInformation (alignedExperiment (r:=d/2-d/8) P) + ENNReal.ofReal (C*d)

def blockInput {d m : ℕ} {U : Type*} (z : (Sphere d × U) × Rows m d) :
    U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ)) :=
  (z.1.2, z.2, label z.2 (z.1.1 : Vector d))

lemma measurable_blockInput {d m : ℕ} {U : Type*} [MeasurableSpace U] :
    Measurable (blockInput (d:=d) (m:=m) (U:=U)) := by
  exact measurable_fst.snd.prodMk (measurable_snd.prodMk
    ((measurable_own_label (d:=d) (k:=m)).comp
      (measurable_snd.prodMk measurable_fst.fst)))

def blockLaw {d m : ℕ} {U V : Type*} [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]
    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U))
    (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) :
    Measure (((Sphere d × U) × Rows m d) × V) :=
  (P.prod (gaussianRows m d)) ⊗ₘ κ.comap blockInput measurable_blockInput

instance blockLaw_probability {d m : ℕ} {U V : Type*}
    [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]
    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) [IsMarkovKernel κ] :
    IsProbabilityMeasure (blockLaw P κ) := by
  unfold blockLaw
  infer_instance

def endingSignalLaw {d m : ℕ} {U V : Type*}
    [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]
    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U))
    (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) : Measure (Sphere d × V) :=
  (blockLaw P κ).map (fun z => (z.1.1.1, z.2))

instance endingSignalLaw_probability {d m : ℕ} {U V : Type*}
    [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]
    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) [IsMarkovKernel κ] :
    IsProbabilityMeasure (endingSignalLaw P κ) := by
  unfold endingSignalLaw
  infer_instance

def freshInformation {d : ℕ} {U : Type*} [MeasurableSpace U] [StandardBorelSpace U]
    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsFiniteMeasure P] (k : ℕ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  exposedInformation (P.prod (gaussianRows k d))

def hybridFirst (d : ℕ) : ℕ := (d+3)/4

def hybridLast (d : ℕ) : ℕ := d/2

def hybridCount (d : ℕ) : ℕ := hybridLast d - hybridFirst d + 1

def averagedFreshInformation {d : ℕ} {U : Type*}
    [MeasurableSpace U] [StandardBorelSpace U]
    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsFiniteMeasure P] : ℝ :=
  (∑ k ∈ Finset.Icc (hybridFirst d) (hybridLast d), (freshInformation P k).toReal) /
    hybridCount d

def HybridBlockMain : Prop :=
  ∃ (C C' : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < C ∧ 0 < C' ∧ ∀ d, d₀ ≤ d →
    ∀ m, 1 ≤ m → m ≤ d/8 →
    ∀ (U V : Type) [Fintype U] [Fintype V]
      [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]
      [MeasurableSingletonClass U] [MeasurableSingletonClass V],
    ∀ (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],
    P.fst = uniformSphere d →
    ∀ (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) [IsMarkovKernel κ],
    ∀ (B : ℝ), 0 ≤ B →
    shannonEntropy (endingSignalLaw P κ).snd ≤ ENNReal.ofReal B →
    averagedFreshInformation (endingSignalLaw P κ) - averagedFreshInformation P ≤
      C*m + (m:ℝ) / hybridCount d * (C + B / (d/8:ℕ)) + B / (d/32:ℕ) ∧
    (B ≤ (d:ℝ)^2 →
      averagedFreshInformation (endingSignalLaw P κ) - averagedFreshInformation P ≤ C'*d)

def FiberBlockMain : Prop :=
  ∃ (K : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < K ∧ ∀ d, d₀ ≤ d →
    ∀ (U V : Type) [Fintype U] [Fintype V]
      [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]
      [MeasurableSingletonClass U] [MeasurableSingletonClass V],
    ∀ (P : Measure (Sphere d×U)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],
    P.fst = uniformSphere d →
    ∀ (κ : Kernel (U×(Rows (d/10) d×(Fin (d/10)→ℝ))) V) [IsMarkovKernel κ],
    ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N → Fintype.card V ≤ N → Real.log N ≤ (d:ℝ)^2 →
    freshInformation (endingSignalLaw P κ) (d/3) ≤ freshInformation P (d/3) + ENNReal.ofReal (K*d)

def dependentMapKernel {A B D : Type*} [MeasurableSpace A] [MeasurableSpace B]
    [MeasurableSpace D] (κ : Kernel A B) (f : A×B → D) : Kernel A D :=
  (Kernel.id ×ₖ κ).map f

def fiberObservationLaw (d k : ℕ) : Measure ((Rows k d×(Fin k→ℝ))×Sphere d) :=
  ((gaussianRows k d).prod (uniformSphere d)).map
    (fun z => ((z.1,label z.1 (z.2:Vector d)),z.2))

instance uniformSphere_finite (d : ℕ) : IsFiniteMeasure (uniformSphere d) := by
  constructor
  unfold uniformSphere
  rw [Measure.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  exact lt_of_le_of_lt (ENNReal.inv_mul_le_one _) ENNReal.one_lt_top

instance fiberObservationLaw_finite (d k : ℕ) : IsFiniteMeasure (fiberObservationLaw d k) := by
  unfold fiberObservationLaw
  infer_instance

lemma sphere_standardBorel (d : ℕ) : StandardBorelSpace (Sphere d) :=
  Metric.isClosed_sphere.measurableSet.standardBorel

def sphericalFiberKernel (d k : ℕ) [sphereNonempty : Nonempty (Sphere d)] :
    Kernel (Rows k d×(Fin k→ℝ)) (Sphere d) :=
  @Measure.condKernel (Rows k d×(Fin k→ℝ)) (Sphere d) _ _
    (sphere_standardBorel d) sphereNonempty
    (fiberObservationLaw d k) (fiberObservationLaw_finite d k)

instance sphericalFiberKernel_markov (d k : ℕ) [Nonempty (Sphere d)] :
    IsMarkovKernel (sphericalFiberKernel d k) := by
  unfold sphericalFiberKernel
  infer_instance

def fiberTwoPointLeft (d k : ℕ) [Nonempty (Sphere d)] : Measure (Rows k d×(Sphere d×Sphere d)) :=
  ((((gaussianRows k d).prod (uniformSphere d)).withDensity
    (fun z => exactDensity (uniformSphere d) z.1 (label z.1 (z.2:Vector d)))) ⊗ₘ
    ((sphericalFiberKernel d k).comap (fun z => (z.1,label z.1 (z.2:Vector d)))
      (measurable_fst.prodMk measurable_own_label))).map
    (fun z => (z.1.1,(z.1.2,z.2)))

def perpendicularRow {d : ℕ} (v g : Vector d) : Vector d :=
  letI : NormedAddCommGroup (Vector d) := PiLp.normedAddCommGroup 2 (fun _ : Fin d => ℝ)
  letI : InnerProductSpace ℝ (Vector d) := PiLp.innerProductSpace (fun _ : Fin d => ℝ)
  g - (⟪v,g⟫ / ‖v‖^2) • v

def perpendicularRowsKernel (d k : ℕ) : Kernel (Sphere d×Sphere d) (Rows k d) :=
  dependentMapKernel (Kernel.const (Sphere d×Sphere d) (gaussianRows k d))
    (fun z => fun i => perpendicularRow ((z.1.1:Vector d)-(z.1.2:Vector d)) (z.2 i))

def fiberTwoPointConstant (k : ℕ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  letI : OfNat ℝ 2 := @instOfNatAtLeastTwo ℝ 2 Real.instNatCast inferInstance
  letI : Mul ℝ := Real.instMul
  letI : Inv ℝ := Real.instInv
  letI : Pow ℝ ℕ := @NPow.toPow ℝ Real.instMonoid.toNPow
  ENNReal.ofReal ((Real.sqrt (2*Real.pi))⁻¹ ^ k)

def fiberDistanceWeight {d : ℕ} (k : ℕ) (z : Sphere d×Sphere d) : ℝ≥0∞ :=
  (ENNReal.ofReal ‖(z.1:Vector d)-(z.2:Vector d)‖)⁻¹ ^ k

def fiberTwoPointRight (d k : ℕ) : Measure (Rows k d×(Sphere d×Sphere d)) :=
  letI : IsScalarTower ℝ≥0∞ ℝ≥0∞ ℝ≥0∞ := Semigroup.isScalarTower
  fiberTwoPointConstant k •
    Measure.map (Prod.swap : (Sphere d×Sphere d)×Rows k d → Rows k d×(Sphere d×Sphere d))
      (Measure.compProd
        (Measure.withDensity (Measure.prod (uniformSphere d) (uniformSphere d))
          (fiberDistanceWeight k))
        (perpendicularRowsKernel d k))

lemma uniformSphere_probability_of_pos {d : ℕ} (hd : 0 < d) :
    IsProbabilityMeasure (uniformSphere d) := by
  let : Nonempty (Fin d) := ⟨⟨0, hd⟩⟩
  constructor
  rw [uniformSphere, Measure.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul]
  apply ENNReal.inv_mul_cancel _ (measure_ne_top _ _)
  rw [Measure.toSphere_apply_univ]
  exact mul_ne_zero
    (by exact_mod_cast (Module.finrank_pos (R := ℝ) (M := Vector d)).ne')
    (Metric.measure_ball_pos volume 0 zero_lt_one).ne'


def FiberTwoPointMain : Prop :=
  ∀ (d k : ℕ) (_hk : 1 ≤ k) (hkd : k < d-1),
    letI : IsProbabilityMeasure (uniformSphere d) :=
      uniformSphere_probability_of_pos (by omega)
    letI : Nonempty (Sphere d) := nonempty_of_isProbabilityMeasure (uniformSphere d)
    IsFiniteMeasure (fiberTwoPointLeft d k) ∧ IsFiniteMeasure (fiberTwoPointRight d k) ∧
      fiberTwoPointLeft d k = fiberTwoPointRight d k

theorem mixedMomentMain : MixedMomentMain := by
  sorry

theorem actualRowMain {d m r q : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    [Countable W] [MeasurableSingletonClass W]
    (hdim : AdmissibleDimensions d m r q)
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (ν : Measure (Sphere d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure ν]
    (hInd : (P.map swapLast).fst = ν.prod (gaussianRows m d))
    (H : ℝ) (hν : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal H • uniformSphere d)
    (hEntropy : shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd ≠ ∞)
    (ρ : PositiveMessage (P.map swapLast).snd → Measure (Sphere d))
    [∀ w, IsFiniteMeasure (ρ w)]
    (_hρac : ∀ w, ρ w ≪ uniformSphere d)
    (B D : PositiveMessage (P.map swapLast).snd → ℝ)
    (hB : ∀ w, 0 < B w) (hD : ∀ w, 0 < D w)
    (hgB : ∀ w, LocalGrowth (ρ w) (m+r) (B w))
    (hgD : ∀ w, LocalGrowth (ρ w) (d-1) (D w))
    (hCI : Integrable (fun sw : Sphere d×W => Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d
      (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd B 1 sw.2) (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd D 1 sw.2))) P.fst) :
    ActualRowConclusion (r:=r) (q:=q) P (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd ρ 0)
      (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd B 1) (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd D 1) := by
  sorry

theorem actualRowRefinement {d m : ℕ} {W E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    [MeasurableSpace E] [Countable W] [MeasurableSingletonClass W]
    [Countable E] [MeasurableSingletonClass E]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (e : Sphere d×W → E) (he : Measurable e)
    (_hEntropy : shannonEntropy
      ((P.map (fun p => ((p.1.1,(p.1.2,e p.1)),p.2))).map swapLast).snd ≠ ∞) :
    rowInformation (P.map (fun p => ((p.1.1,(p.1.2,e p.1)),p.2))) (gaussianRows m d) =
      rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d) := by
  sorry

theorem actualRowSourceMain {d m r q : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    [Countable W] [MeasurableSingletonClass W]
    (hdim : AdmissibleDimensions d m r q)
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (ν : Measure (Sphere d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure ν]
    (hInd : (P.map swapLast).fst = ν.prod (gaussianRows m d))
    (H : ℝ) (hν : ν ≤ ENNReal.ofReal H • uniformSphere d)
    (hEntropy : shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd ≠ ∞)
    (ρ : PositiveMessage (P.map swapLast).snd → Measure (Sphere d))
    [∀ w, IsFiniteMeasure (ρ w)]
    (_hρac : ∀ w, ρ w ≪ uniformSphere d)
    (B D : PositiveMessage (P.map swapLast).snd → ℝ)
    (hB : ∀ w, 0 < B w) (hD : ∀ w, 0 < D w)
    (hgB : ∀ w, LocalGrowth (ρ w) (m+r) (B w))
    (hgD : ∀ w, LocalGrowth (ρ w) (d-1) (D w))
    (hCI : Integrable (fun sw : Sphere d×W => Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d
      (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd B 1 sw.2) (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd D 1 sw.2))) P.fst) :
    ActualRowSourceConclusion (r:=r) (q:=q) P (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd ρ 0)
      (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd B 1) (completeFamily (P.map swapLast).snd D 1) := by
  sorry

theorem actualRowSourceRefinement {d m : ℕ} {W E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]
    [MeasurableSpace E] [Countable W] [MeasurableSingletonClass W]
    [Countable E] [MeasurableSingletonClass E]
    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
    (ν : Measure (Sphere d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure ν]
    (hInd : (P.map swapLast).fst = ν.prod (gaussianRows m d))
    (e : Sphere d×W → E) (he : Measurable e)
    (hEntropy : shannonEntropy
      ((P.map (fun p => ((p.1.1,(p.1.2,e p.1)),p.2))).map swapLast).snd ≠ ∞) :
    actualConditionalRowInformation (P.map (fun p => ((p.1.1,(p.1.2,e p.1)),p.2))) =
      actualConditionalRowInformation P := by
  sorry

theorem criticalComparisonMain : CriticalComparisonMain := by
  sorry

theorem oneLevelComparisonMain : OneLevelComparisonMain := by
  sorry

theorem twoLabelComparisonMain : TwoLabelComparisonMain := by
  sorry

theorem hybridBlockMain : HybridBlockMain := by
  sorry

theorem fiberTwoPointMain : FiberTwoPointMain := by
  sorry

theorem fiberBlockMain : FiberBlockMain := by
  sorry

end CurrentProjection

end

end OAI
```

```json
      "statement_lean": "def gaussianRows (k d : ℕ) : Measure (Rows k d) :=\n  Measure.pi fun _ => stdGaussian (Vector d)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "label",
      "statement_lean": "def label {k d : ℕ} (g : Rows k d) (s : Vector d) : Fin k → ℝ :=\n  fun i => ⟪s, g i⟫"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "LocalGrowth",
      "statement_lean": "def LocalGrowth {d : ℕ} (ρ : Measure (Sphere d)) (a : ℕ) (B : ℝ) : Prop :=\n  ∀ z : Vector d, ∀ t : ℝ, 0 < t →\n    ρ {u | dist (u : Vector d) z ≤ t} ≤ ENNReal.ofReal (B * t ^ a)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "cubeAverage",
      "statement_lean": "def cubeAverage {k d : ℕ} (ρ : Measure (Sphere d)) (g : Rows k d)\n    (y : Fin k → ℝ) (δ : ℝ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  ENNReal.ofReal ((2 * δ)⁻¹ ^ k) *\n    ρ {u | ∀ i, |label g (u : Vector d) i - y i| ≤ δ}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "exactDensity",
      "statement_lean": "def exactDensity {k d : ℕ} (ρ : Measure (Sphere d)) (g : Rows k d)\n    (y : Fin k → ℝ) : ℝ≥0∞ := by\n  classical\n  let f := fun j : ℕ => (cubeAverage ρ g y (1 / (j : ℝ))).toReal\n  exact if h : ∃ L : ℝ, Tendsto f atTop (𝓝 L) then ENNReal.ofReal h.choose else 0"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "AdmissibleDimensions",
      "statement_lean": "def AdmissibleDimensions (d m r q : ℕ) : Prop :=\n  1 ≤ m ∧ 1 ≤ r ∧ 1 ≤ q ∧ m + r < d - 1 ∧ m + r - m - (q - 1) ≥ 1"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "momentConstant",
      "statement_lean": "def momentConstant (C : ℝ) (d : ℕ) (B D : ℝ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (C * d) * B * (1 + max (Real.log (D / B)) 0))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "MixedMomentMain",
      "statement_lean": "def MixedMomentMain : Prop :=\n  ∃ C : ℝ, 0 < C ∧ ∀ (d m r q : ℕ), AdmissibleDimensions d m r q →\n    ∀ (ρ : Measure (Sphere d)), IsFiniteMeasure ρ →\n    ∀ (B D : ℝ), 0 < B → 0 < D → LocalGrowth ρ (m+r) B → LocalGrowth ρ (d-1) D →\n    (∀ (s : Sphere d) (δ : ℝ), 0 < δ →\n      (∫⁻ X, (∫⁻ Z, cubeAverage ρ (Fin.append X Z)\n        (label (Fin.append X Z) (s : Vector d)) δ ∂gaussianRows r d)^q\n          ∂gaussianRows m d) ≤ (momentConstant C d B D)^q) ∧\n    (ρ ≪ uniformSphere d → ∃ E : Set (Sphere d), MeasurableSet E ∧ ρ E = 0 ∧\n      ∀ s ∉ E,\n        (∀ᵐ G ∂gaussianRows (m+r) d,\n          Tendsto (fun δ : ℝ => cubeAverage ρ G (label G (s : Vector d)) δ)\n            (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 (exactDensity ρ G (label G (s : Vector d))))) ∧\n        ((∫⁻ X, (∫⁻ Z, exactDensity ρ (Fin.append X Z)\n          (label (Fin.append X Z) (s : Vector d)) ∂gaussianRows r d)^q\n            ∂gaussianRows m d) ≤ (momentConstant C d B D)^q) ∧\n        (ρ ≠ 0 → ∀ᵐ G ∂gaussianRows (m+r) d,\n          0 < exactDensity ρ G (label G (s : Vector d)) ∧\n          exactDensity ρ G (label G (s : Vector d)) < ∞))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "gaussianRows_isProbabilityMeasure",
      "statement_lean": "instance gaussianRows_isProbabilityMeasure (k d : ℕ) : IsProbabilityMeasure (gaussianRows k d) := by\n  unfold gaussianRows\n  infer_instance"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "lemma",
      "name": "measurable_own_label",
      "statement_lean": "lemma measurable_own_label {k d : ℕ} :\n    Measurable (fun z : Rows k d × Sphere d => label z.1 (z.2:Vector d)) := by\n  apply Measurable.of_eval\n  intro i\n  exact (continuous_inner.comp <| (continuous_subtype_val.comp continuous_snd).prodMk\n    ((continuous_apply i).comp continuous_fst)).measurable"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "swapLast",
      "statement_lean": "def swapLast {A B C : Type*} [MeasurableSpace A] [MeasurableSpace B] [MeasurableSpace C] :\n    (A × B) × C ≃ᵐ (A × C) × B where\n  toFun p := ((p.1.1,p.2),p.1.2)\n  invFun p := ((p.1.1,p.2),p.1.2)\n  left_inv _ := rfl\n  right_inv _ := rfl\n  measurable_toFun := (measurable_fst.fst.prodMk measurable_snd).prodMk measurable_fst.snd\n  measurable_invFun := (measurable_fst.fst.prodMk measurable_snd).prodMk measurable_fst.snd"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "rowInformation",
      "statement_lean": "def rowInformation {S W X : Type*} [MeasurableSpace S] [MeasurableSpace W]\n    [MeasurableSpace X] (P : Measure ((S×W)×X)) (γ : Measure X) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  klDiv P (P.fst.prod γ)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "shannonEntropy",
      "statement_lean": "noncomputable def shannonEntropy {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W] (π : Measure W) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  ∫⁻ w, ENNReal.ofReal (-Real.log (π {w}).toReal) ∂π"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "conditionalMutualInformation",
      "statement_lean": "def conditionalMutualInformation {Z A B : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Z]\n    [MeasurableSpace A] [MeasurableSpace B] [StandardBorelSpace A] [StandardBorelSpace B]\n    (P : Measure (Z×(A×B))) [IsFiniteMeasure P] : ℝ≥0∞ := by\n  classical\n  exact if h : Nonempty (A×B) then\n    letI := h\n    InformationTheory.klDiv P (P.fst ⊗ₘ (P.condKernel.fst ×ₖ P.condKernel.snd))\n  else 0"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "actualExperiment",
      "statement_lean": "def actualExperiment {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d × W) × Rows m d)) :\n    Measure (((Sphere d × W) × Rows m d) × Rows r d) := P.prod (gaussianRows r d)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "actualExperiment_isProbabilityMeasure",
      "statement_lean": "instance actualExperiment_isProbabilityMeasure {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] :\n    IsProbabilityMeasure (actualExperiment (r:=r) P) :=\n  inferInstanceAs (IsProbabilityMeasure (P.prod (gaussianRows r d)))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "actualObservation",
      "statement_lean": "def actualObservation {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*}\n    (p : ((Sphere d × W) × Rows m d) × Rows r d) :\n    (Rows (m+r) d × W) × (Fin (m+r) → ℝ) :=\n  ((Fin.append p.1.2 p.2,p.1.1.2), label (Fin.append p.1.2 p.2) (p.1.1.1 : Vector d))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "actualObservation_isProbabilityMeasure",
      "statement_lean": "instance actualObservation_isProbabilityMeasure {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] :\n    IsProbabilityMeasure ((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation) :=\n  inferInstance"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "selectedCube",
      "statement_lean": "def selectedCube {k d : ℕ} {W : Type*} (ρ : W → Measure (Sphere d)) (δ : ℝ)\n    (z : (Rows k d×W)×(Fin k → ℝ)) : ℝ≥0∞ := cubeAverage (ρ z.1.2) z.1.1 z.2 δ"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "selectedExact",
      "statement_lean": "def selectedExact {k d : ℕ} {W : Type*} (ρ : W → Measure (Sphere d))\n    (z : (Rows k d×W)×(Fin k → ℝ)) : ℝ≥0∞ := exactDensity (ρ z.1.2) z.1.1 z.2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "momentBound",
      "statement_lean": "def momentBound (C : ℝ) (d : ℕ) (B D : ℝ) : ℝ :=\n  Real.exp (C*d)*B*(1+max (Real.log (D/B)) 0)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "logPositive",
      "statement_lean": "def logPositive (x : ℝ≥0∞) : ℝ := max (Real.log x.toReal) 0"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "logNegative",
      "statement_lean": "def logNegative (x : ℝ≥0∞) : ℝ≥0∞ := if x = 0 then ∞ else ENNReal.ofReal (-Real.log x.toReal)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "extendedLogExpectation",
      "statement_lean": "def extendedLogExpectation {A : Type*} [MeasurableSpace A] (μ : Measure A)\n    (f : A → ℝ≥0∞) : EReal :=\n  ((∫⁻ a, ENNReal.ofReal (logPositive (f a)) ∂μ) : EReal) -\n    ((∫⁻ a, logNegative (f a) ∂μ) : EReal)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "PositiveMessage",
      "statement_lean": "def PositiveMessage {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W] (π : Measure W) := {w : W // 0 < π {w}}"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "completeFamily",
      "statement_lean": "def completeFamily {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W] {T : Type*} (π : Measure W)\n    (f : PositiveMessage π → T) (t₀ : T) (w : W) : T :=\n  if h : 0 < π {w} then f ⟨w,h⟩ else t₀"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "structure",
      "name": "ActualRowConclusion",
      "statement_lean": "structure ActualRowConclusion {d m r q : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (ρ : W → Measure (Sphere d)) (B D : W → ℝ) : Prop where\n  row_finite : rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d) ≠ ∞\n  row_le_entropy : (rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal ≤\n    (shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd).toReal\n  conditional_density : ∀ᵐ gw ∂((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation).fst,\n    ((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation).condKernel gw ≪ volume\n  actual_limit : ∀ᵐ p ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P,\n    Tendsto (fun j : ℕ => selectedCube ρ (1/(j:ℝ)) (actualObservation p)) atTop\n      (𝓝 (selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)))\n  smoothed_bound : ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ →\n    Integrable (fun p => Real.log (1+(selectedCube ρ δ (actualObservation p)).toReal))\n      (actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ∧\n    (∫ p, Real.log (1+(selectedCube ρ δ (actualObservation p)).toReal) ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ≤\n      (rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal/q +\n        ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst\n  exact_integrable : Integrable (fun p => Real.log (1+(selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)).toReal))\n    (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)\n  exact_bound : (∫ p, Real.log (1+(selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)).toReal) ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ≤\n    (rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal/q +\n      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst\n  entropy_bound : (rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal/q +\n      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst ≤\n    (shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd).toReal/q +\n      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst\n  positive_log_integrable : Integrable (fun p => logPositive (selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)))\n    (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)\n  extended_log_bound : extendedLogExpectation (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)\n    (fun p => selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)) ≤\n      (((rowInformation P (gaussianRows m d)).toReal/q +\n        ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst) : EReal)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "actualConditionalRowInformation",
      "statement_lean": "def actualConditionalRowInformation {d m : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]\n    [StandardBorelSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsFiniteMeasure P] : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  conditionalMutualInformation (P.map (fun p => (p.1.1,(p.2,p.1.2))))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "structure",
      "name": "ActualRowSourceConclusion",
      "statement_lean": "structure ActualRowSourceConclusion {d m r q : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]\n    [StandardBorelSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (ρ : W → Measure (Sphere d)) (B D : W → ℝ) : Prop where\n  row_finite : actualConditionalRowInformation P ≠ ∞\n  row_le_entropy : (actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal ≤\n    (shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd).toReal\n  conditional_density : ∀ᵐ gw ∂((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation).fst,\n    ((actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map actualObservation).condKernel gw ≪ volume\n  actual_limit : ∀ᵐ p ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P,\n    Tendsto (fun j : ℕ => selectedCube ρ (1/(j:ℝ)) (actualObservation p)) atTop\n      (𝓝 (selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)))\n  smoothed_bound : ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ →\n    Integrable (fun p => Real.log (1+(selectedCube ρ δ (actualObservation p)).toReal))\n      (actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ∧\n    (∫ p, Real.log (1+(selectedCube ρ δ (actualObservation p)).toReal) ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ≤\n      (actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal/q +\n        ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst\n  exact_integrable : Integrable (fun p => Real.log (1+(selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)).toReal))\n    (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)\n  exact_bound : (∫ p, Real.log (1+(selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)).toReal) ∂actualExperiment (r:=r) P) ≤\n    (actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal/q +\n      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst\n  entropy_bound : (actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal/q +\n      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst ≤\n    (shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd).toReal/q +\n      ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst\n  positive_log_integrable : Integrable (fun p => logPositive (selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)))\n    (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)\n  extended_log_bound : extendedLogExpectation (actualExperiment (r:=r) P)\n    (fun p => selectedExact ρ (actualObservation p)) ≤\n      (((actualConditionalRowInformation P).toReal/q +\n        ∫ sw : Sphere d×W, Real.log (1+momentBound 9 d (B sw.2) (D sw.2)) ∂P.fst) : EReal)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "alignedExperiment",
      "statement_lean": "def alignedExperiment {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows (m+r) d) :=\n  (actualExperiment (r:=r) P).map (fun p => (p.1.1,Fin.append p.1.2 p.2))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "alignedExperiment_probability",
      "statement_lean": "instance alignedExperiment_probability {d m r : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows m d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P] :\n    IsProbabilityMeasure (alignedExperiment (r:=r) P) := by\n  unfold alignedExperiment\n  infer_instance"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "exposedJointMap",
      "statement_lean": "def exposedJointMap {d k : ℕ} {W : Type*} (p : (Sphere d×W)×Rows k d) :\n    (Rows k d×(Fin k → ℝ))×(Sphere d×W) := ((p.2,label p.2 (p.1.1:Vector d)),p.1)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "exposedInformation",
      "statement_lean": "def exposedInformation {d k : ℕ} {W : Type*} [MeasurableSpace W] [StandardBorelSpace W]\n    (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows k d)) [IsFiniteMeasure P] : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  conditionalMutualInformation (P.map exposedJointMap)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "CriticalComparisonMain",
      "statement_lean": "def CriticalComparisonMain : Prop :=\n  ∃ (K : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < K ∧ ∀ (d : ℕ), d₀ ≤ d →\n    ∀ (W : Type) [MeasurableSpace W] [Countable W] [MeasurableSingletonClass W],\n    ∀ (P : Measure ((Sphere d×W)×Rows (d/10) d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],\n    (P.map swapLast).fst = (uniformSphere d).prod (gaussianRows (d/10) d) →\n    shannonEntropy (P.map swapLast).snd ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((d:ℝ)^2) →\n    exposedInformation (P.fst.prod (gaussianRows (d/10+(d/2-d/10)) d)) ≤\n      exposedInformation (alignedExperiment (r:=d/2-d/10) P) + ENNReal.ofReal (K*d)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "OneLevelComparisonMain",
      "statement_lean": "def OneLevelComparisonMain : Prop :=\n  ∃ (C : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < C ∧ ∀ d, d₀ ≤ d →\n    ∀ (U : Type) [MeasurableSpace U] [Finite U] [MeasurableSingletonClass U],\n    ∀ (P : Measure ((Sphere d×U)×Rows (d/32) d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],\n    (P.map swapLast).fst = (uniformSphere d).prod (gaussianRows (d/32) d) →\n    exposedInformation (P.fst.prod (gaussianRows (d/32+(d/4-d/32)) d)) ≤\n      exposedInformation (alignedExperiment (r:=d/4-d/32) P) +\n      ENNReal.ofReal (C*((d:ℝ)+Real.log (1+(d:ℝ)+(shannonEntropy P.fst.snd).toReal)+\n        (shannonEntropy P.fst.snd).toReal/(d:ℝ)))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "TwoLabelComparisonMain",
      "statement_lean": "def TwoLabelComparisonMain : Prop :=\n  ∃ (C : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < C ∧ ∀ d, d₀ ≤ d →\n    ∀ (V : Type) [MeasurableSpace V] [Finite V] [MeasurableSingletonClass V],\n    ∀ (P : Measure ((Sphere d×V)×Rows (d/8) d)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],\n    (P.map swapLast).fst = (uniformSphere d).prod (gaussianRows (d/8) d) →\n    shannonEntropy P.fst.snd ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ((d:ℝ)^2) →\n    exposedInformation (P.fst.prod (gaussianRows (d/8+(d/2-d/8)) d)) ≤\n      exposedInformation (alignedExperiment (r:=d/2-d/8) P) + ENNReal.ofReal (C*d)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "blockInput",
      "statement_lean": "def blockInput {d m : ℕ} {U : Type*} (z : (Sphere d × U) × Rows m d) :\n    U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ)) :=\n  (z.1.2, z.2, label z.2 (z.1.1 : Vector d))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "lemma",
      "name": "measurable_blockInput",
      "statement_lean": "lemma measurable_blockInput {d m : ℕ} {U : Type*} [MeasurableSpace U] :\n    Measurable (blockInput (d:=d) (m:=m) (U:=U)) := by\n  exact measurable_fst.snd.prodMk (measurable_snd.prodMk\n    ((measurable_own_label (d:=d) (k:=m)).comp\n      (measurable_snd.prodMk measurable_fst.fst)))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "blockLaw",
      "statement_lean": "def blockLaw {d m : ℕ} {U V : Type*} [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]\n    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U))\n    (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) :\n    Measure (((Sphere d × U) × Rows m d) × V) :=\n  (P.prod (gaussianRows m d)) ⊗ₘ κ.comap blockInput measurable_blockInput"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "blockLaw_probability",
      "statement_lean": "instance blockLaw_probability {d m : ℕ} {U V : Type*}\n    [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]\n    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) [IsMarkovKernel κ] :\n    IsProbabilityMeasure (blockLaw P κ) := by\n  unfold blockLaw\n  infer_instance"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "endingSignalLaw",
      "statement_lean": "def endingSignalLaw {d m : ℕ} {U V : Type*}\n    [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]\n    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U))\n    (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) : Measure (Sphere d × V) :=\n  (blockLaw P κ).map (fun z => (z.1.1.1, z.2))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "endingSignalLaw_probability",
      "statement_lean": "instance endingSignalLaw_probability {d m : ℕ} {U V : Type*}\n    [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]\n    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]\n    (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) [IsMarkovKernel κ] :\n    IsProbabilityMeasure (endingSignalLaw P κ) := by\n  unfold endingSignalLaw\n  infer_instance"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "freshInformation",
      "statement_lean": "def freshInformation {d : ℕ} {U : Type*} [MeasurableSpace U] [StandardBorelSpace U]\n    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsFiniteMeasure P] (k : ℕ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  exposedInformation (P.prod (gaussianRows k d))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "hybridFirst",
      "statement_lean": "def hybridFirst (d : ℕ) : ℕ := (d+3)/4"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "hybridLast",
      "statement_lean": "def hybridLast (d : ℕ) : ℕ := d/2"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "hybridCount",
      "statement_lean": "def hybridCount (d : ℕ) : ℕ := hybridLast d - hybridFirst d + 1"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "averagedFreshInformation",
      "statement_lean": "def averagedFreshInformation {d : ℕ} {U : Type*}\n    [MeasurableSpace U] [StandardBorelSpace U]\n    (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsFiniteMeasure P] : ℝ :=\n  (∑ k ∈ Finset.Icc (hybridFirst d) (hybridLast d), (freshInformation P k).toReal) /\n    hybridCount d"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "HybridBlockMain",
      "statement_lean": "def HybridBlockMain : Prop :=\n  ∃ (C C' : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < C ∧ 0 < C' ∧ ∀ d, d₀ ≤ d →\n    ∀ m, 1 ≤ m → m ≤ d/8 →\n    ∀ (U V : Type) [Fintype U] [Fintype V]\n      [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]\n      [MeasurableSingletonClass U] [MeasurableSingletonClass V],\n    ∀ (P : Measure (Sphere d × U)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],\n    P.fst = uniformSphere d →\n    ∀ (κ : Kernel (U × (Rows m d × (Fin m → ℝ))) V) [IsMarkovKernel κ],\n    ∀ (B : ℝ), 0 ≤ B →\n    shannonEntropy (endingSignalLaw P κ).snd ≤ ENNReal.ofReal B →\n    averagedFreshInformation (endingSignalLaw P κ) - averagedFreshInformation P ≤\n      C*m + (m:ℝ) / hybridCount d * (C + B / (d/8:ℕ)) + B / (d/32:ℕ) ∧\n    (B ≤ (d:ℝ)^2 →\n      averagedFreshInformation (endingSignalLaw P κ) - averagedFreshInformation P ≤ C'*d)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "FiberBlockMain",
      "statement_lean": "def FiberBlockMain : Prop :=\n  ∃ (K : ℝ) (d₀ : ℕ), 0 < K ∧ ∀ d, d₀ ≤ d →\n    ∀ (U V : Type) [Fintype U] [Fintype V]\n      [MeasurableSpace U] [MeasurableSpace V]\n      [MeasurableSingletonClass U] [MeasurableSingletonClass V],\n    ∀ (P : Measure (Sphere d×U)) [IsProbabilityMeasure P],\n    P.fst = uniformSphere d →\n    ∀ (κ : Kernel (U×(Rows (d/10) d×(Fin (d/10)→ℝ))) V) [IsMarkovKernel κ],\n    ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N → Fintype.card V ≤ N → Real.log N ≤ (d:ℝ)^2 →\n    freshInformation (endingSignalLaw P κ) (d/3) ≤ freshInformation P (d/3) + ENNReal.ofReal (K*d)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "dependentMapKernel",
      "statement_lean": "def dependentMapKernel {A B D : Type*} [MeasurableSpace A] [MeasurableSpace B]\n    [MeasurableSpace D] (κ : Kernel A B) (f : A×B → D) : Kernel A D :=\n  (Kernel.id ×ₖ κ).map f"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "fiberObservationLaw",
      "statement_lean": "def fiberObservationLaw (d k : ℕ) : Measure ((Rows k d×(Fin k→ℝ))×Sphere d) :=\n  ((gaussianRows k d).prod (uniformSphere d)).map\n    (fun z => ((z.1,label z.1 (z.2:Vector d)),z.2))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "uniformSphere_finite",
      "statement_lean": "instance uniformSphere_finite (d : ℕ) : IsFiniteMeasure (uniformSphere d) := by\n  constructor\n  unfold uniformSphere\n  rw [Measure.smul_apply, smul_eq_mul]\n  exact lt_of_le_of_lt (ENNReal.inv_mul_le_one _) ENNReal.one_lt_top"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "fiberObservationLaw_finite",
      "statement_lean": "instance fiberObservationLaw_finite (d k : ℕ) : IsFiniteMeasure (fiberObservationLaw d k) := by\n  unfold fiberObservationLaw\n  infer_instance"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "lemma",
      "name": "sphere_standardBorel",
      "statement_lean": "lemma sphere_standardBorel (d : ℕ) : StandardBorelSpace (Sphere d) :=\n  Metric.isClosed_sphere.measurableSet.standardBorel"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "sphericalFiberKernel",
      "statement_lean": "def sphericalFiberKernel (d k : ℕ) [sphereNonempty : Nonempty (Sphere d)] :\n    Kernel (Rows k d×(Fin k→ℝ)) (Sphere d) :=\n  @Measure.condKernel (Rows k d×(Fin k→ℝ)) (Sphere d) _ _\n    (sphere_standardBorel d) sphereNonempty\n    (fiberObservationLaw d k) (fiberObservationLaw_finite d k)"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "instance",
      "name": "sphericalFiberKernel_markov",
      "statement_lean": "instance sphericalFiberKernel_markov (d k : ℕ) [Nonempty (Sphere d)] :\n    IsMarkovKernel (sphericalFiberKernel d k) := by\n  unfold sphericalFiberKernel\n  infer_instance"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "fiberTwoPointLeft",
      "statement_lean": "def fiberTwoPointLeft (d k : ℕ) [Nonempty (Sphere d)] : Measure (Rows k d×(Sphere d×Sphere d)) :=\n  ((((gaussianRows k d).prod (uniformSphere d)).withDensity\n    (fun z => exactDensity (uniformSphere d) z.1 (label z.1 (z.2:Vector d)))) ⊗ₘ\n    ((sphericalFiberKernel d k).comap (fun z => (z.1,label z.1 (z.2:Vector d)))\n      (measurable_fst.prodMk measurable_own_label))).map\n    (fun z => (z.1.1,(z.1.2,z.2)))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "perpendicularRow",
      "statement_lean": "def perpendicularRow {d : ℕ} (v g : Vector d) : Vector d :=\n  letI : NormedAddCommGroup (Vector d) := PiLp.normedAddCommGroup 2 (fun _ : Fin d => ℝ)\n  letI : InnerProductSpace ℝ (Vector d) := PiLp.innerProductSpace (fun _ : Fin d => ℝ)\n  g - (⟪v,g⟫ / ‖v‖^2) • v"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "perpendicularRowsKernel",
      "statement_lean": "def perpendicularRowsKernel (d k : ℕ) : Kernel (Sphere d×Sphere d) (Rows k d) :=\n  dependentMapKernel (Kernel.const (Sphere d×Sphere d) (gaussianRows k d))\n    (fun z => fun i => perpendicularRow ((z.1.1:Vector d)-(z.1.2:Vector d)) (z.2 i))"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "fiberTwoPointConstant",
      "statement_lean": "def fiberTwoPointConstant (k : ℕ) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  letI : OfNat ℝ 2 := @instOfNatAtLeastTwo ℝ 2 Real.instNatCast inferInstance\n  letI : Mul ℝ := Real.instMul\n  letI : Inv ℝ := Real.instInv\n  letI : Pow ℝ ℕ := @NPow.toPow ℝ Real.instMonoid.toNPow\n  ENNReal.ofReal ((Real.sqrt (2*Real.pi))⁻¹ ^ k)"

    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "fiberDistanceWeight",
      "statement_lean": "def fiberDistanceWeight {d : ℕ} (k : ℕ) (z : Sphere d×Sphere d) : ℝ≥0∞ :=\n  (ENNReal.ofReal ‖(z.1:Vector d)-(z.2:Vector d)‖)⁻¹ ^ k"
    },
    {
      "docstring": null,
      "kind": "def",
      "name": "fiberTwoPointRight",
      "statement_lean": "def fiberTwoPointRight (d k : ℕ) : Measure (Rows k d×(Sphere d×Sphere d)) :=\n  letI : IsScalarTower ℝ≥0∞ ℝ≥0∞ ℝ≥0∞ := Semigroup.isScalarTower\n  fiberTwoPointConstant k •\n    Measure.map (Prod.swap : (Sphere d×Sphere d)×Rows k d → Rows k d×(Sphere d×Sphere d))\n      (Measure.compProd\n        (Measure.withDensity (Measure.prod (uniformSphere d) (uniformSphere d))\n          (fiberDistanceWeight k))\n        (perpendicularRowsKernel d k))"
```
